# Each file of this folder, the file of the session's scratch tree it was copied from (the path below the scratch
# root), that source's sha256, and what the copy changed:
#   prefix      the absolute session-scratch prefix replaced by 'scratchpad' (or 'session')
#   links       Markdown links to the session's checkout (/home/user/mesozoic-labs/...) made relative to the
#               repository root
#   whitespace  trailing whitespace removed and one final newline added, as the repository's pre-commit hooks require
#   trailer     two commit-trailer lines replaced by a placeholder
#   gzip        the result compressed (Python gzip.compress, level 9, mtime 0); the copy is <name>.gz, gunzip restores it
#   none        byte-identical
# Nothing else differs.  Format: <file> <source> <source sha256> <changes>; a source whose path holds spaces is
# written in double quotes.  The folders added on 2026-10-18 rename the scratch's logs/ directories run_logs/ or
# judge_logs/ (the repository's .gitignore ignores every directory named logs).
# Added 2026-10-19 (stance3/results/): four more changes, nothing else:
#   excerpt     one section of a Markdown file, verbatim (its heading to the line before the next section)
#   trim        a JSON file cut to the keys its folder's README names, written with indent 1
#   extract     the value of one key of a JSON file, written on its own with indent 1
#   redact      the session's tool identifiers each replaced by '[id removed]' (a bare workflow run id by
#               'workflow [id removed]'): in stance3/results/run_log.txt, 12 workflow run ids (11 distinct)
#               and the id prefix of the report document
# A source outside the scratch root is written below 'session' (the session directory above it), with a task
# id in its path replaced by '[id removed]'.  The run log's source was appended to after it was copied: the
# sha256 given is that of its first 221 lines, the whole file when copied.
